#### `void BM_OLAP_MultiJoin(benchmark::State &state)`
- Source: `benchmarks/analytics/bench_olap_analytics.cpp`:211
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `state` (benchmark::State &): n/a
- Details: Hash-join across 3 tables (orders × customers × products) with GROUP BY aggregation. Simulates: SELECT c.region_id, p.category_id, SUM(o.price * o.product_id) FROM orders o JOIN customers c ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id JOIN products p ON o.product_id = p.product_id GROUP BY c.region_id, p.category_id Target: ≥100 K rows/sec at 100 K orders

#### `void BM_OLAP_TopN_Sorted(benchmark::State &state)`
- Source: `benchmarks/analytics/bench_olap_analytics.cpp`:276
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `state` (benchmark::State &): n/a
- Details: Top-N query: per-group aggregation followed by ORDER BY DESC LIMIT N. Simulates: SELECT product_id, SUM(amount) AS total FROM sales GROUP BY product_id ORDER BY total DESC LIMIT 10 Target: ≥300 K rows/sec at 1 M rows

#### `std::vector< DataPoint > makeBinaryData(int n, int seed=0)`
- Source: `tests/analytics/test_automl.cpp`:43
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `n` (int): n/a
  - `seed` (int): n/a
- Details: Build a binary classification dataset: two linearly separable clusters. Class "pos" when sum(features) > 0, "neg" otherwise.

#### `std::vector< DataPoint > makeBinaryThresholdData(int n)`
- Source: `tests/analytics/test_automl.cpp`:776
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `n` (int): n/a
- Details: Build a binary classification dataset: class = (x > 0.5 ? 1 : 0). When used as a regression proxy the expected output for x just above 0.5 should be close to 1.0, and for x just below 0.5 it should be close to 0.0.

#### `std::vector< DataPoint > makeLinear2xData(int n)`
- Source: `tests/analytics/test_automl.cpp`:678
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `n` (int): n/a
- Details: Build a dataset with a single feature x and target y = 2*x. x is uniformly spread over [0, 10] with n points. Requires n >= 2 to produce a meaningful linear spread.

#### `std::vector< DataPoint > makeLinearData(int n)`
- Source: `tests/analytics/test_automl.cpp`:592
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `n` (int): n/a
- Details: Build a simple 1-D regression dataset: y = 2 * x, x in [0, 1]. The KNN regressor (inverse-distance-weighted mean of k=5 nearest neighbours) must predict a value close to 2 * query_x.

#### `std::vector< DataPoint > makeMulticlassData(int n)`
- Source: `tests/analytics/test_automl.cpp`:65
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `n` (int): n/a
- Details: Build a multi-class dataset with 3 classes based on sine quadrants.

#### `std::vector< DataPoint > makeRegressionData(int n, int seed=0)`
- Source: `tests/analytics/test_automl.cpp`:84
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `n` (int): n/a

- Details: Build a regression dataset: y = 2*x1 - x2 + noise.

#### `TEST(LLMProcessAnalyzerPerfTest, PutInCache_O1Eviction_Under1us)`
- Source: `tests/analytics/test_llm_process_analyzer.cpp`:720
- Brief: Verify that the full analyze() call with LRU eviction completes in ≤ 100 µs P99 when the cache is at capacity (1 000 entries).
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (LLMProcessAnalyzerPerfTest): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (PutInCache_O1Eviction_Under1us): n/a
- Details: Gates on the entire analyze() round-trip (getCacheKey + cache miss + callLLM stub + putInCache with LRU eviction). The eviction itself is O(1) and contributes negligibly; the 100 µs ceiling gives ample CI headroom while still catching an O(N) regression. This test is timing-sensitive and opt-in via THEMIS_RUN_PERF_TESTS=1.

#### `void * allocate(size_t size, size_t align=alignof(std::max_align_t))`
- Source: `include/analytics/detail/memory_pool.h`:89
- Brief: Allocate size bytes aligned to align from the arena.
- Parameters:
  - `size` (size_t): Number of bytes to allocate (must be > 0).
  - `align` (size_t): Required alignment (must be a power of two, ≥ 1).
- Return: Pointer to the allocated region. Valid until reset() is called.
- Throws:
  - std::bad_alloc: if an overflow slab cannot be obtained.
- Details: size Number of bytes to allocate (must be > 0). align Required alignment (must be a power of two, ≥ 1). Pointer to the allocated region. Valid until reset() is called. std::bad_alloc if an overflow slab cannot be obtained.

#### `void reset() noexcept`
- Source: `include/analytics/detail/memory_pool.h`:100
- Brief: Reset the arena, making all previously allocated memory available for reuse without releasing the primary backing storage.
- Parameters: none
- Details: Call this at the start of each query / execute() invocation. Overflow slabs (if any) are freed to avoid unbounded memory growth.

#### `void * try_alloc(uint8_t *base, size_t &cursor, size_t cap, size_t size, size_t align) noexcept`
- Source: `include/analytics/detail/memory_pool.h`:186
- Brief: Try to allocate size bytes with align from the block described by (base, cursor, cap).
- Parameters:
  - `base` (uint8_t *): n/a
  - `cursor` (size_t &): n/a
  - `cap` (size_t): n/a
  - `size` (size_t): n/a
  - `align` (size_t): n/a
- Return: Pointer on success, nullptr if the block is too small.
- Details: Alignment is computed on the absolute pointer address so that the returned pointer satisfies the alignment contract for any power-of-two alignment, regardless of how base itself was aligned. Pointer on success, nullptr if the block is too small.

#### `size_t used() const noexcept`
- Source: `include/analytics/detail/memory_pool.h`:109
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters: none
- Return: Number of bytes currently consumed across primary and overflow blocks.
- Details: Number of bytes currently consumed across primary and overflow blocks.

### themis::bench::ana_bm

#### `void BM_ANA_BM01_TimeSeriesIngestP95(benchmark::State &state)`
- Source: `benchmarks/analytics/bench_analytics_dedicated_gates.cpp`:146
- Brief: ANA-BM-01: Single-event ingest into a 64-slot tumbling window.
- Parameters:
  - `state` (benchmark::State &): n/a
- Details: Measures the per-event ingest cost: accumulate value into partial sum, increment count, check window boundary. GATE-ANA-BM-01: p95 ≤ 2 µs per event.

#### `void BM_ANA_BM02_ColumnarScanP95(benchmark::State &state)`
- Source: `benchmarks/analytics/bench_analytics_dedicated_gates.cpp`:180
- Brief: ANA-BM-02: Predicate evaluation over a 256-row int64 batch.
- Parameters:
  - `state` (benchmark::State &): n/a
- Details: Measures the per-batch columnar scan cost: sequential predicate evaluation over 256 deterministic int64 values. GATE-ANA-BM-02: p95 ≤ 10 µs per batch.

#### `void BM_ANA_BM03_AggregationThroughput(benchmark::State &state)`
- Source: `benchmarks/analytics/bench_analytics_dedicated_gates.cpp`:215
- Brief: ANA-BM-03: SUM over 1000 double values with NaN guard.
- Parameters:
  - `state` (benchmark::State &): n/a
- Details: Measures the aggregation pipeline throughput for a 1000-element double accumulation — the dedicated analytics-specific equivalent of ARG-01 (which used int64). GATE-ANA-BM-03: ≥ 10 000 ops/sec (each iteration = 1000 values). At 1 GHz pipeline this is trivially met; gate is a regression floor.

#### `void BM_ANA_BM04_WindowRollupP99(benchmark::State &state)`
- Source: `benchmarks/analytics/bench_analytics_dedicated_gates.cpp`:248
- Brief: ANA-BM-04: Full tumbling window cycle — 64 adds → flush → reset.
- Parameters:
  - `state` (benchmark::State &): n/a
- Details: Measures the end-to-end window rollup cost: accumulate 64 events into the partial sum, trigger the flush boundary (partial_sum reset + count reset), then report the flush. GATE-ANA-BM-04: p99 ≤ 5 µs per flush cycle.

#### `bool restoreFromCheckpoint(const std::string &checkpoint_id)`
- Source: `include/analytics/cep_engine.h`:1136
- Brief: Restore From Checkpoint.
- Parameters:
  - `checkpoint_id` (const std::string &): Identifier of the checkpoint.
- Return: True when the operation succeeds.
- Details: Parses the text checkpoint produced by createCheckpoint(). checkpoint_id Identifier of the checkpoint. True when the operation succeeds. checkpoint_id Identifier of the checkpoint. True when the operation succeeds. "rule=<id>:<name>:<1\|0>" lines restore the enabled/disabled state of each rule. "pm_rule=" / "pm_rule_end" blocks restore the NFA partial match state of each pattern matcher, allowing in-progress stateful pattern sequences to survive a shutdown/restart cycle. Counter lines (events_received=, etc.) are intentionally skipped because they are cumulative since engine initialisation and cannot be meaningfully restored. Calls: std::filesystem::path(), std::filesystem::exists(), spdlog::error(), string(), spdlog::info(), f(), is_open(), std::getline().

#### `void * allocate(size_t size, size_t align=alignof(std::max_align_t))`
- Source: `include/analytics/detail/memory_pool.h`:89
- Brief: Allocate size bytes aligned to align from the arena.
- Parameters:
  - `size` (size_t): Number of bytes to allocate (must be > 0).
  - `align` (size_t): Required alignment (must be a power of two, ≥ 1).
- Return: Pointer to the allocated region. Valid until reset() is called.
- Throws:
  - std::bad_alloc: if an overflow slab cannot be obtained.
- Details: size Number of bytes to allocate (must be > 0). align Required alignment (must be a power of two, ≥ 1). Pointer to the allocated region. Valid until reset() is called. std::bad_alloc if an overflow slab cannot be obtained.

#### `void reset() noexcept`
- Source: `include/analytics/detail/memory_pool.h`:100
- Brief: Reset the arena, making all previously allocated memory available for reuse without releasing the primary backing storage.
- Parameters: none
- Details: Call this at the start of each query / execute() invocation. Overflow slabs (if any) are freed to avoid unbounded memory growth.

#### `void * try_alloc(uint8_t *base, size_t &cursor, size_t cap, size_t size, size_t align) noexcept`
- Source: `include/analytics/detail/memory_pool.h`:186
- Brief: Try to allocate size bytes with align from the block described by (base, cursor, cap).
- Parameters:
  - `base` (uint8_t *): n/a
  - `cursor` (size_t &): n/a
  - `cap` (size_t): n/a
  - `size` (size_t): n/a
  - `align` (size_t): n/a
- Return: Pointer on success, nullptr if the block is too small.
- Details: Alignment is computed on the absolute pointer address so that the returned pointer satisfies the alignment contract for any power-of-two alignment, regardless of how base itself was aligned. Pointer on success, nullptr if the block is too small.
